                unsafe { self.0.assume_init_ref() }.len()
            }
            fn set_len(&mut self, len: usize) {
                unsafe { self.0.assume_init_mut() }.set_len(len)
            }
        }
        const _: () = {
            impl crate::__private::FieldOffset
            for __FieldToken<A28, Vec<(u8, String, String)>, 0>
            where
                [(); __field_offset()]:,
            {
                type Offset = crate::__private::typenum::Const<{ __field_offset() }>;
            }
            pub const fn __field_offset() -> usize {
                {
                    use core::mem::MaybeUninit;
                    let origin: MaybeUninit<A28> = MaybeUninit::uninit();
                    #[allow(unused_variables)]
                    let A28 { vec } = unsafe { origin.assume_init_ref() };
                    unsafe {
                        crate::__private::sub_ptr(
                            vec as *const _ as *const u8,
                            origin.assume_init_ref() as *const _ as *const u8,
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        };
    };
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::fmt::Debug for A28 {
        #[inline]
        fn fmt(&self, f: &mut ::core::fmt::Formatter) -> ::core::fmt::Result {
            ::core::fmt::Formatter::debug_struct_field1_finish(
                f,
                "A28",
                "vec",
                &&self.vec,
            )
        }
    }
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::cmp::Eq for A28 {
        #[inline]
        #[doc(hidden)]
        #[coverage(off)]
        fn assert_receiver_is_total_eq(&self) -> () {
            let _: ::core::cmp::AssertParamIsEq<Vec<(u8, String, String)>>;
        }
    }
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::marker::StructuralPartialEq for A28 {}
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::cmp::PartialEq for A28 {
        #[inline]
        fn eq(&self, other: &A28) -> bool {
            self.vec == other.vec
        }
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    mod tests {
        use std::{
            any::type_name, convert::Infallible, fmt::Debug, marker::PhantomData,
            str::FromStr,
        };
        use crate::{
            mock::BinaryCodecMock,
            prelude::{Mesh, SegmentCodec, SegmentDecoder, SegmentEncoder, SegmentWalker},
            Buffer,
        };
        use super::*;
        extern crate test;
        #[cfg(test)]
        #[rustc_test_marker = "fuzz::tests::test_fuzzing"]
        #[doc(hidden)]
        pub const test_fuzzing: test::TestDescAndFn = test::TestDescAndFn {
            desc: test::TestDesc {
                name: test::StaticTestName("fuzz::tests::test_fuzzing"),
                ignore: false,
                ignore_message: ::core::option::Option::None,
                source_file: "src/fuzz.rs",
                start_line: 148usize,
                start_col: 8usize,
                end_line: 148usize,
                end_col: 20usize,
                compile_fail: false,
                no_run: false,
                should_panic: test::ShouldPanic::No,
                test_type: test::TestType::UnitTest,
            },
            testfn: test::StaticTestFn(
                #[coverage(off)]
                || test::assert_test_result(test_fuzzing()),
            ),
        };
        fn test_fuzzing() {
            test(A1);
            test(A2 {});
            test(A3 {});
            test(A4(123));
            test(A5 { v: 123 });
            test(A6 { v1: 11, v2: 22 });
            test(A7(11, 22));
            test(A8::A);
            test(A9::B);
            test(A10::A(123));
            test(A10::A(123));
            test(A11::A(11, 22));
            test(A12::B(11, 22));
            test(A13::C);
            test(A18 { value: 123 });
            test(A20::E);
            test(A22::A { value: 123 });
            test(A23 {
                value: <[_]>::into_vec(::alloc::boxed::box_new([123])),
            });
            test(A24 {
                value: <[_]>::into_vec(
                    ::alloc::boxed::box_new([String::from_str("hi").unwrap()]),
                ),
            });
            test(A25 {
                value: <[_]>::into_vec(
                    ::alloc::boxed::box_new([
                        A26 {
                            value1: 11,
                            value2: String::from_str("hihi").unwrap(),
                            value3: 22,
                        },
                    ]),
                ),
            });
            test(A27 {
                value: (11, 22),
                value2: (
                    11,
                    String::from_str("hello").unwrap(),
                    String::from_str("hihi").unwrap(),
                ),
            });
            test(A28 {
                vec: <[_]>::into_vec(
                    ::alloc::boxed::box_new([
                        (
                            1,
                            String::from_str("HIHIHIHHIH").unwrap(),
                            String::from_str("asdfasdfa").unwrap(),
                        ),
                    ]),
                ),
            });
        }
        fn test<T: Eq + Debug>(value: T)
        where
            T: Mesh<BinaryCodecMock, SegmentEncoder>,
            T: Mesh<BinaryCodecMock, SegmentDecoder>,
            [(); size_of::<T>()]:,
        {
            let mut dst = [0u8; 100000];
            crate::mock::encode(&value, &mut dst).unwrap();
            {
                ::std::io::_print(format_args!("decode start\n"));
            };
            let decoded = crate::mock::decode::<T>(&mut dst).unwrap();
            {
                ::std::io::_print(format_args!("decode end\n"));
            };
            match (&value, &decoded) {
                (left_val, right_val) => {
                    if !(*left_val == *right_val) {
                        let kind = ::core::panicking::AssertKind::Eq;
                        ::core::panicking::assert_failed(
                            kind,
                            &*left_val,
                            &*right_val,
                            ::core::option::Option::None,
                        );
                    }
                }
            };
        }
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod mock {
    use std::mem::{discriminant, transmute, Discriminant, MaybeUninit};
    use crate::{
        prelude::{walk_segment, Mesh, SegmentDecoder, SegmentEncoder},
        BufRead, BufWrite, Buffer, Codec, CompositeDecoder, CompositeEncoder, Decode,
        Decoder, Encoder, Endian, EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex,
        EnumIdentifierToVariantIndexError, EnumVariantDiscriminantId, EnumVariantIndex,
        EnumVariantStringId,
    };
    pub struct BinaryCodecMock {
        buffer: Buffer,
    }
    impl Codec for BinaryCodecMock {
        fn endian(&self) -> Endian {
            Endian::NATIVE
        }
    }
    impl BufWrite for BinaryCodecMock {
        fn write_array<T: Copy, const N: usize>(&mut self, src: &[T; N]) {
            self.buffer.write_array::<T, N>(src)
        }
        fn write_slice<T: Copy>(&mut self, src: &[T]) {
            self.buffer.write_slice::<T>(src)
        }
    }
    pub fn encode<'a, T>(
        src: &T,
        dst: &mut [u8],
    ) -> Result<(), <BinaryCodecMock as Encoder>::Error>
    where
        T: Mesh<BinaryCodecMock, SegmentEncoder>,
    {
        let buffer = Buffer::from(dst);
        let mut codec = BinaryCodecMock { buffer };
        let src: *const T = &*src;
        walk_segment::<T, BinaryCodecMock, SegmentEncoder>(src, &mut codec)
    }
    pub fn decode<'a, T>(src: &[u8]) -> Result<T, <BinaryCodecMock as Decoder>::Error>
    where
        T: Mesh<BinaryCodecMock, SegmentDecoder>,
    {
        let mut out = MaybeUninit::uninit();
        let buffer = Buffer::from(src);
        let mut codec = BinaryCodecMock { buffer };
        walk_segment::<T, BinaryCodecMock, SegmentDecoder>(out.as_ptr(), &mut codec)?;
        Ok(unsafe { out.assume_init() })
    }
    impl Encoder for BinaryCodecMock
    where
        Self: BufWrite,
    {

        type TupleEncoder = Self;
        type StructEncoder = Self;
        type SequenceEncoder = Self;

            Ok(())
        }

            self.write_slice(v);
            Ok(())

            if v < 255 {
                self.encode_u8(&(v as u8))?;
            } else {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        fn encode_enum_identifier<T>(
            &mut self,
            value: &T,
        ) -> Result<EnumVariantIndex, Self::Error>
        where
            [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:,
            for<'a> &'a T: Into<EnumVariantStringId>
                + Into<EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>>,
            T: EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantStringId>
                + EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>>,
        {
            let id: EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T> = value.into();
            self.write_array(&id);
            let result = T::enum_variant_index_by_identifier(id);
            Ok(unsafe { result.unwrap_unchecked() })
        }
    }

    pub enum EncodeError {

        TooLarge,
        Custom,

                    EncodeError::TooLarge => "TooLarge",

        fn custom() -> Self {
            Self::Custom
        }
    }

            ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
        }
        fn decode_seq_len(&mut self) -> Result<usize, Self::Error> {
            let mut read = MaybeUninit::uninit();
            self.decode_u8(&mut read)?;
            let read = unsafe { read.assume_init() };
            Ok(
                if read < 255 {
                    read as usize
                } else {
                    ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
                },
            )
        }
        fn decode_is_some(&mut self) -> Result<bool, Self::Error> {
            ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
        }
        fn decode_enum_identifier<T>(
            &mut self,
            out: &mut MaybeUninit<T>,
        ) -> Result<EnumVariantIndex, Self::Error>
        where
            T: EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantStringId>
                + EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>>,
            [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:,
        {
            let mut discriminant: MaybeUninit<[u8; size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]> = MaybeUninit::uninit();
            self.read_array(&mut discriminant);
            let id = EnumVariantDiscriminantId::from(unsafe {
                discriminant.assume_init()
            });
            T::enum_variant_index_by_identifier(id)
                .map_err(|EnumIdentifierToVariantIndexError::InvalidIdentifier| {
                    crate::DecodeError::invalid_enum_identifier()
                })
        }
    }

        }

            self.buffer.read_slice::<T>(out)
        }
    }

                f,

                    DecodeError::NotEnoughBytesInTheBuffer => "NotEnoughBytesInTheBuffer",

                    DecodeError::Custom => "Custom",
                    DecodeError::InvalidUtf8 => "InvalidUtf8",

                    DecodeError::NonZeroButZero => "NonZeroButZero",
                },
            )
        }
    }

                        &mut self,
                        out: &mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<Address>,
                    ) {
                        self.x0
                            .decode_in_place(unsafe {
                                &mut *(&raw mut (*out.as_mut_ptr()).x0
                                    as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<u8>)
                            });
                        self.x1
                            .decode_in_place(unsafe {
                                &mut *(&raw mut (*out.as_mut_ptr()).x1
                                    as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<u8>)
                            });
                        self.x2
                            .decode_in_place(unsafe {
                                &mut *(&raw mut (*out.as_mut_ptr()).x2
                                    as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<u8>)
                            });
                        self.x3
                            .decode_in_place(unsafe {
                                &mut *(&raw mut (*out.as_mut_ptr()).x3
                                    as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<u8>)
                            });
                    }
                }
            };
            impl Default for Logs {
                fn default() -> Self {
                    Self {
                        logs: ::alloc::vec::from_elem(
                            Log {
                                address: Address {
                                    x0: 11,
                                    x1: 22,
                                    x2: 33,
                                    x3: 44,
                                },
                                identity: String::from_str("abcd").unwrap(),
                                userid: String::from_str("a").unwrap(),
                                date: String::from_str("wijkl").unwrap(),
                                request: String::from_str("mnop").unwrap(),
                                code: 55,
                                size: 66,
                            },
                            10_000,
                        ),
                    }
                }
            }

            #[ignore]
            fn bench_log_model_encode(b: &mut Bencher) {
                let model = Logs::default();
                let mut dst = unsafe { Box::<[u8; 1000000]>::new_uninit().assume_init() }
                    as Box<[u8]>;
                black_box(&model);
                b.iter(|| {
                    black_box(encode(&model, &mut dst).unwrap());
                });
                {
                    ::std::io::_print(format_args!("{0:?}\n", &dst[..66]));
                };
                black_box(&dst);
            }

            #[ignore]
            fn bench_log_model_with_decode_bitcode(b: &mut Bencher) {
                let model = Logs::default();
                black_box(&model);
                let mut buf = bitcode::Buffer::default();
                let encoded: Vec<u8> = black_box(&buf.encode(&model))
                    .iter()
                    .cloned()
                    .collect();
                b.iter(|| black_box(buf.decode::<Logs>(&encoded)));
                black_box(&buf);
            }

mod buffer {
    use std::mem::{transmute, MaybeUninit};
    pub struct Buffer {
        pub(crate) ptr: *mut u8,
        pub(crate) len: usize,
    }
    pub trait BufWrite {
        fn write_array<T: Copy, const N: usize>(&mut self, src: &[T; N]);
        fn write_slice<T: Copy>(&mut self, src: &[T]);
    }
    pub trait BufRead {
        fn read_array<T: Copy, const N: usize>(&mut self, out: &mut MaybeUninit<[T; N]>);
        fn read_slice<T: Copy>(&mut self, out: &mut [MaybeUninit<T>]);
    }
    impl From<&[u8]> for Buffer {
        fn from(value: &[u8]) -> Self {
            Self {
                ptr: value.as_ptr() as *const _ as *mut u8,
                len: value.len(),
            }
        }
    }
    impl From<&mut [u8]> for Buffer {
        fn from(value: &mut [u8]) -> Self {
            Self {
                ptr: value.as_mut_ptr(),
                len: value.len(),
            }
        }
    }
    pub const CHUNK_SIZE: usize = if true { 16 } else { 4 };
    impl BufWrite for Buffer {
        fn write_array<T: Copy, const N: usize>(&mut self, src: &[T; N]) {
            let dst = self.ptr as *mut T;
            self.ptr = dst.wrapping_add(N) as *mut u8;
            let src = src.as_ptr();
            unsafe {
                if true {
                    if !(N != 0 && N <= N) {
                        ::core::panicking::panic("assertion failed: N != 0 && N <= N")
                    }
                }
                let page_size = 4096;
                let read_size = core::mem::size_of::<[T; N]>();
                let within_page = src as usize & (page_size - 1)
                    < (page_size - read_size) && false;
                if within_page {
                    *(dst
                        as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<
                            [T; N],
                        >) = core::ptr::read(
                        src as *const core::mem::MaybeUninit<[T; N]>,
                    );
                } else {
                    src.copy_to_nonoverlapping(dst, N);
                };
            }
        }
        fn write_slice<T: Copy>(&mut self, src: &[T]) {
            let mut iter = src.chunks_exact(CHUNK_SIZE);
            loop {
                let chunk = match iter.next() {
                    Some(v) => v,
                    None => {
                        let remainder = iter.remainder();
                        let dst = self.ptr as *mut T;
                        let src = remainder.as_ptr();
                        unsafe {
                            if true {
                                if !(CHUNK_SIZE != 0 && CHUNK_SIZE <= CHUNK_SIZE) {
                                    ::core::panicking::panic(
                                        "assertion failed: CHUNK_SIZE != 0 && CHUNK_SIZE <= CHUNK_SIZE",
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            let page_size = 4096;
                            let read_size = core::mem::size_of::<[T; CHUNK_SIZE]>();
                            let within_page = src as usize & (page_size - 1)
                                < (page_size - read_size) && false;
                            if within_page {
                                *(dst
                                    as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<
                                        [T; CHUNK_SIZE],
                                    >) = core::ptr::read(
                                    src as *const core::mem::MaybeUninit<[T; CHUNK_SIZE]>,
                                );
                            } else {
                                src.copy_to_nonoverlapping(dst, CHUNK_SIZE);
                            };
                        }
                        self.ptr = dst.wrapping_add(remainder.len()) as *mut u8;
                        break;
                    }
                };
                let dst = self.ptr as *mut T;
                unsafe {
                    let src = chunk.as_ptr();
                    if true {
                        if !(CHUNK_SIZE != 0 && CHUNK_SIZE <= CHUNK_SIZE) {
                            ::core::panicking::panic(
                                "assertion failed: CHUNK_SIZE != 0 && CHUNK_SIZE <= CHUNK_SIZE",
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    let page_size = 4096;
                    let read_size = core::mem::size_of::<[T; CHUNK_SIZE]>();
                    let within_page = src as usize & (page_size - 1)
                        < (page_size - read_size) && false;
                    if within_page {
                        *(dst
                            as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<
                                [T; CHUNK_SIZE],
                            >) = core::ptr::read(
                            src as *const core::mem::MaybeUninit<[T; CHUNK_SIZE]>,
                        );
                    } else {
                        src.copy_to_nonoverlapping(dst, CHUNK_SIZE);
                    };
                }
                self.ptr = dst.wrapping_add(CHUNK_SIZE) as *mut u8;
            }
        }
    }
    impl BufRead for Buffer {
        fn read_slice<T: Copy>(&mut self, out: &mut [MaybeUninit<T>]) {
            let mut iter = out.chunks_exact_mut(CHUNK_SIZE);
            loop {
                let chunk = match iter.next() {
                    Some(v) => v,
                    None => {
                        let remainder = iter.into_remainder();
                        unsafe {
                            for v in remainder.into_iter() {
                                let src = self.ptr as *const T;
                                self.ptr = src.wrapping_add(1) as *mut u8;
                                let dst = v.as_ptr() as *mut T;
                                if true {
                                    if !(1 != 0 && 1 <= 1) {
                                        ::core::panicking::panic(
                                            "assertion failed: 1 != 0 && 1 <= 1",
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                                let page_size = 4096;
                                let read_size = core::mem::size_of::<[T; 1]>();
                                let within_page = src as usize & (page_size - 1)
                                    < (page_size - read_size) && false;
                                if within_page {
                                    *(dst
                                        as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<
                                            [T; 1],
                                        >) = core::ptr::read(
                                        src as *const core::mem::MaybeUninit<[T; 1]>,
                                    );
                                } else {
                                    src.copy_to_nonoverlapping(dst, 1);
                                };
                            }
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                };
                let src = self.ptr as *const T;
                let dst = chunk.as_ptr() as *mut T;
                unsafe {
                    if true {
                        if !(CHUNK_SIZE != 0 && CHUNK_SIZE <= CHUNK_SIZE) {
                            ::core::panicking::panic(
                                "assertion failed: CHUNK_SIZE != 0 && CHUNK_SIZE <= CHUNK_SIZE",
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    let page_size = 4096;
                    let read_size = core::mem::size_of::<[T; CHUNK_SIZE]>();
                    let within_page = src as usize & (page_size - 1)
                        < (page_size - read_size) && false;
                    if within_page {
                        *(dst
                            as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<
                                [T; CHUNK_SIZE],
                            >) = core::ptr::read(
                            src as *const core::mem::MaybeUninit<[T; CHUNK_SIZE]>,
                        );
                    } else {
                        src.copy_to_nonoverlapping(dst, CHUNK_SIZE);
                    };
                }
                self.ptr = src.wrapping_add(CHUNK_SIZE) as *mut u8;
            }
        }
        fn read_array<T: Copy, const N: usize>(
            &mut self,
            out: &mut MaybeUninit<[T; N]>,
        ) {
            let src = self.ptr as *const T;
            let dst = out.as_mut_ptr() as *mut T;
            self.ptr = src.wrapping_add(N) as *mut u8;
            unsafe {
                if true {
                    if !(N != 0 && N <= N) {
                        ::core::panicking::panic("assertion failed: N != 0 && N <= N")
                    }
                }
                let page_size = 4096;
                let read_size = core::mem::size_of::<[T; N]>();
                let within_page = src as usize & (page_size - 1)
                    < (page_size - read_size) && false;
                if within_page {
                    *(dst
                        as *mut core::mem::MaybeUninit<
                            [T; N],
                        >) = core::ptr::read(
                        src as *const core::mem::MaybeUninit<[T; N]>,
                    );
                } else {
                    src.copy_to_nonoverlapping(dst, N);
                };
            }
        }
    }
}
mod codec {
    #[cfg(feature = "bin")]
    pub mod bin {
        use std::{
            mem::{transmute, transmute_copy, Discriminant, MaybeUninit},
            rc::Weak,
        };
        use crate::{
            prelude::{walk_segment, Mesh, SegmentDecoder, SegmentEncoder},
            BufRead, BufWrite, Buffer, CompositeDecoder, CompositeEncoder, Decode,
            Decoder, Encode, Encoder, Endian,
        };
        use super::{
            Codec, EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex, EnumIdentifierToVariantIndexError,
            EnumVariantDiscriminantId, EnumVariantIndex, EnumVariantStringId,
        };
        pub struct BinaryCodec {
            buffer: Buffer,
        }
        pub fn encode<'a, T>(
            src: &T,
            dst: &mut [u8],
        ) -> Result<(), <BinaryCodec as Encoder>::Error>
        where
            T: Mesh<BinaryCodec, SegmentEncoder>,
        {
            let buffer = Buffer::from(dst);
            let ref mut codec = BinaryCodec { buffer };
            walk_segment::<T, BinaryCodec, SegmentEncoder>(src, codec);
            Ok(())
        }
        pub fn decode<'a, T>(src: &[u8]) -> Result<T, <BinaryCodec as Decoder>::Error>
        where
            T: Mesh<BinaryCodec, SegmentDecoder>,
        {
            let out = MaybeUninit::uninit();
            let buffer = Buffer::from(src);
            let mut codec = BinaryCodec { buffer };
            walk_segment::<T, BinaryCodec, SegmentDecoder>(out.as_ptr(), &mut codec)?;
            Ok(unsafe { out.assume_init() })
        }
        impl BufWrite for BinaryCodec {
            fn write_array<T: Copy, const N: usize>(&mut self, src: &[T; N]) {
                self.buffer.write_array::<T, N>(src);
            }
            fn write_slice<T: Copy>(&mut self, src: &[T]) {
                self.buffer.write_slice::<T>(src);
            }
        }
        impl BufRead for BinaryCodec {
            fn read_array<T: Copy, const N: usize>(
                &mut self,
                out: &mut MaybeUninit<[T; N]>,
            ) {
                self.buffer.read_array::<T, N>(out);
            }
            fn read_slice<T: Copy>(&mut self, out: &mut [MaybeUninit<T>]) {
                self.buffer.read_slice::<T>(out)
            }
        }
        impl Codec for BinaryCodec {
            fn endian(&self) -> crate::Endian {
                Endian::NATIVE
            }
        }
        impl Encoder for BinaryCodec
        where
            Self: BufWrite,
        {
            type Error = EncodeError;
            type TupleEncoder = Self;
            type StructEncoder = Self;
            type SequenceEncoder = Self;
            fn encode_u8(&mut self, v: &u8) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                self.buffer.write_array(&v.to_ne_bytes());
                Ok(())
            }
            fn encode_i8(&mut self, v: &i8) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_u16(&mut self, v: &u16) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                self.write_array(
                    &match self.endian() {
                        Endian::Big => v.to_be_bytes(),
                        Endian::Little => v.to_le_bytes(),
                    },
                );
                Ok(())
            }
            fn encode_i16(&mut self, v: &i16) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_u32(&mut self, v: &u32) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                self.write_array(
                    &match self.endian() {
                        Endian::Big => v.to_be_bytes(),
                        Endian::Little => v.to_le_bytes(),
                    },
                );
                Ok(())
            }
            fn encode_i32(&mut self, v: &i32) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_u64(&mut self, v: &u64) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                self.write_array(
                    &match self.endian() {
                        Endian::Big => v.to_be_bytes(),
                        Endian::Little => v.to_le_bytes(),
                    },
                );
                Ok(())
            }
            fn encode_i64(&mut self, v: &i64) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_u128(&mut self, v: &u128) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_i128(&mut self, v: &i128) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_usize(&mut self, v: &usize) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_isize(&mut self, v: &isize) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_f32(&mut self, v: &f32) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_f64(&mut self, v: &f64) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_bool(&mut self, v: &bool) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_tuple(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self::TupleEncoder, Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_struct(
                &mut self,
            ) -> Result<&mut Self::StructEncoder, Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_seq(
                &mut self,
                len: usize,
            ) -> Result<&mut Self::SequenceEncoder, Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_some(&mut self) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_none(&mut self) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_str(&mut self, v: &str) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_bytes(&mut self, v: &[u8]) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn encode_seq_len(&mut self, v: usize) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                if true {
                    if !(v < u32::MAX as usize) {
                        ::core::panicking::panic(
                            "assertion failed: v < u32::MAX as usize",
                        )
                    }
                }
                self.encode_u64(&(v as u64))
            }
            fn encode_enum_identifier<T>(
                &mut self,
                value: &T,
            ) -> Result<EnumVariantIndex, Self::Error>
            where
                [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:,
                for<'a> &'a T: Into<EnumVariantStringId>
                    + Into<EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>>,
                T: EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantStringId>
                    + EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>>,
            {
                let id: EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T> = value.into();
                self.write_array(&id);
                let result = T::enum_variant_index_by_identifier(id);
                Ok(unsafe { result.unwrap_unchecked() })
            }
        }
        impl CompositeEncoder for BinaryCodec
        where
            Self: BufWrite,
        {
            fn encode_element<E: Encode>(&mut self, v: &E) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                v.encode(self)
            }
            fn end(&mut self) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                Ok(())
            }
        }
        pub enum EncodeError {
            NotEnoughSpaceInTheBuffer,
            TooLarge,
            Custom,
        }
        #[automatically_derived]
        impl ::core::fmt::Debug for EncodeError {
            #[inline]
            fn fmt(&self, f: &mut ::core::fmt::Formatter) -> ::core::fmt::Result {
                ::core::fmt::Formatter::write_str(
                    f,
                    match self {
                        EncodeError::NotEnoughSpaceInTheBuffer => {
                            "NotEnoughSpaceInTheBuffer"
                        }
                        EncodeError::TooLarge => "TooLarge",
                        EncodeError::Custom => "Custom",
                    },
                )
            }
        }
        impl crate::EncodeError for EncodeError {
            fn not_enough_space_in_the_buffer() -> Self {
                Self::NotEnoughSpaceInTheBuffer
            }
            fn too_large() -> Self {
                Self::TooLarge
            }
            fn custom() -> Self {
                Self::Custom
            }
        }
        impl Decoder for BinaryCodec
        where
            Self: BufRead,
        {
            type Error = DecodeError;
            type TupleDecoder = Self;
            type StructDecoder = Self;
            type SequenceDecoder = Self;
            fn decode_u8(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u8>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(unsafe { transmute(place) });
                Ok(())
            }
            fn decode_i8(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i8>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_u16(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u16>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                match self.endian() {
                    Endian::Big => {
                        match Endian::NATIVE {
                            Endian::Big => {
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 2>(unsafe { transmute(place) })
                            }
                            Endian::Little => {
                                let out: &mut [MaybeUninit<[u8; 1]>; 2] = unsafe {
                                    transmute(place)
                                };
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[1]);
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[0]);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Endian::Little => {
                        match Endian::NATIVE {
                            Endian::Big => {
                                let out: &mut [MaybeUninit<[u8; 1]>; 2] = unsafe {
                                    transmute(place)
                                };
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[1]);
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[0]);
                            }
                            Endian::Little => {
                                self.buffer
                                    .read_array::<u8, 2>(unsafe { transmute(place) });
                            }
                        }
                    }
                };
                Ok(())
            }
            fn decode_i16(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i16>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_u32(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u32>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                match self.endian() {
                    Endian::Big => {
                        match Endian::NATIVE {
                            Endian::Big => {
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 2>(unsafe { transmute(place) })
                            }
                            Endian::Little => {
                                let out: &mut [MaybeUninit<[u8; 1]>; 2] = unsafe {
                                    transmute(place)
                                };
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[1]);
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[0]);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Endian::Little => {
                        match Endian::NATIVE {
                            Endian::Big => {
                                let out: &mut [MaybeUninit<[u8; 1]>; 4] = unsafe {
                                    transmute(place)
                                };
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[1]);
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[0]);
                            }
                            Endian::Little => {
                                self.buffer
                                    .read_array::<u8, 4>(unsafe { transmute(place) });
                            }
                        }
                    }
                };
                Ok(())
            }
            fn decode_i32(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i32>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_u64(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u64>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                match self.endian() {
                    Endian::Big => {
                        match Endian::NATIVE {
                            Endian::Big => {
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 2>(unsafe { transmute(place) })
                            }
                            Endian::Little => {
                                let out: &mut [MaybeUninit<[u8; 1]>; 2] = unsafe {
                                    transmute(place)
                                };
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[1]);
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[0]);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Endian::Little => {
                        match Endian::NATIVE {
                            Endian::Big => {
                                let out: &mut [MaybeUninit<[u8; 1]>; 4] = unsafe {
                                    transmute(place)
                                };
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[1]);
                                self.buffer.read_array::<u8, 1>(&mut out[0]);
                            }
                            Endian::Little => {
                                self.buffer
                                    .read_array::<u8, 8>(unsafe { transmute(place) });
                            }
                        }
                    }
                };
                Ok(())
            }
            fn decode_i64(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i64>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_u128(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u128>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_i128(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i128>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_usize(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<usize>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_isize(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<isize>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_f32(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<f32>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_f64(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<f64>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_bool(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<bool>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_str(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut std::mem::MaybeUninit<&str>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_bytes<'a>(&mut self) -> Result<&'a [u8], Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_var_i32(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut std::mem::MaybeUninit<i32>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_tuple(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self::TupleDecoder, Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_struct(
                &mut self,
            ) -> Result<&mut Self::StructDecoder, Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_seq(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self::SequenceDecoder, Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_seq_len(&mut self) -> Result<usize, Self::Error> {
                let mut out = MaybeUninit::uninit();
                self.decode_u64(&mut out)?;
                Ok(unsafe { out.assume_init() } as usize)
            }
            fn decode_is_some(&mut self) -> Result<bool, Self::Error> {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn decode_enum_identifier<T>(
                &mut self,
                out: &mut MaybeUninit<T>,
            ) -> Result<EnumVariantIndex, Self::Error>
            where
                T: EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantStringId>
                    + EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>>,
                [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:,
            {
                let mut discriminant: MaybeUninit<[u8; size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]> = MaybeUninit::uninit();
                self.read_array(&mut discriminant);
                let id = EnumVariantDiscriminantId::from(unsafe {
                    discriminant.assume_init()
                });
                T::enum_variant_index_by_identifier(id)
                    .map_err(|EnumIdentifierToVariantIndexError::InvalidIdentifier| {
                        crate::DecodeError::invalid_enum_identifier()
                    })
            }
        }
        impl CompositeDecoder for BinaryCodec
        where
            Self: BufRead,
        {
            fn decode_element<D: Decode>(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<D>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                D::decode_in_place(self, place)
            }
            fn end(&mut self) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
                Ok(())
            }
        }
        pub enum DecodeError {
            NotEnoughBytesInTheBuffer,
            TooLarge,
            InvalidEnumIdentifier,
            Custom,
        }
        #[automatically_derived]
        impl ::core::fmt::Debug for DecodeError {
            #[inline]
            fn fmt(&self, f: &mut ::core::fmt::Formatter) -> ::core::fmt::Result {
                ::core::fmt::Formatter::write_str(
                    f,
                    match self {
                        DecodeError::NotEnoughBytesInTheBuffer => {
                            "NotEnoughBytesInTheBuffer"
                        }
                        DecodeError::TooLarge => "TooLarge",
                        DecodeError::InvalidEnumIdentifier => "InvalidEnumIdentifier",
                        DecodeError::Custom => "Custom",
                    },
                )
            }
        }
        impl crate::DecodeError for DecodeError {
            fn not_enough_bytes_in_the_buffer() -> Self {
                Self::NotEnoughBytesInTheBuffer
            }
            fn too_large() -> Self {
                Self::TooLarge
            }
            fn invalid_enum_identifier() -> Self {
                Self::InvalidEnumIdentifier
            }
            fn custom() -> Self {
                Self::Custom
            }
            fn invalid_utf8() -> Self {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn nonmax_but_max() -> Self {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
            fn nonzero_but_zero() -> Self {
                ::core::panicking::panic("not yet implemented")
            }
        }

    mod traits {
        use std::{
            mem::{discriminant, transmute, transmute_copy, Discriminant, MaybeUninit},
            ops::{Deref, Index},
        };
        use crate::{BufRead, BufWrite, Endian};
        pub trait Codec {
            fn endian(&self) -> Endian;
        }
        ///It's not enum variant discriminant, but in ordered index number of variant
        #[repr(transparent)]
        pub struct EnumVariantIndex(pub usize);
        ///Use for String codec like json
        #[repr(transparent)]
        pub struct EnumVariantStringId(pub &'static str);
        ///It may contain invalid data
        #[repr(transparent)]
        pub struct EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>(
            [u8; size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()],
        )
        where
            [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:;
        impl<T> EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>
        where
            [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:,
        {
            pub fn new(t: &T) -> Self {
                Self(unsafe { const_transmute(discriminant(t)) })
            }
        }
        const unsafe fn const_transmute<A, B>(a: A) -> B {
            if std::mem::size_of::<A>() != std::mem::size_of::<B>() {
                {
                    ::core::panicking::panic_fmt(
                        format_args!("Size mismatch for generic_array::const_transmute"),
                    );
                };
            }
            #[repr(C)]
            union Union<A, B> {
                a: std::mem::ManuallyDrop<A>,
                b: std::mem::ManuallyDrop<B>,
            }
            let a = std::mem::ManuallyDrop::new(a);
            std::mem::ManuallyDrop::into_inner(Union { a }.b)
        }
        impl<T> Deref for EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>
        where
            [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:,
        {
            type Target = [u8; size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()];
            fn deref(&self) -> &Self::Target {
                &self.0
            }
        }
        impl<T> From<[u8; size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]> for EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>
        where
            [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:,
        {
            fn from(value: [u8; size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]) -> Self {
                Self(value)
            }
        }
        pub trait EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<ID> {
            fn enum_variant_index_by_identifier(
                id: ID,
            ) -> Result<EnumVariantIndex, EnumIdentifierToVariantIndexError>;
        }
        pub enum EnumIdentifierToVariantIndexError {
            InvalidIdentifier,
        }
        #[automatically_derived]
        impl ::core::fmt::Debug for EnumIdentifierToVariantIndexError {
            #[inline]
            fn fmt(&self, f: &mut ::core::fmt::Formatter) -> ::core::fmt::Result {
                ::core::fmt::Formatter::write_str(f, "InvalidIdentifier")
            }
        }
        pub trait Encode {
            fn encode<E: Encoder>(&self, encoder: &mut E) -> Result<(), E::Error>;
        }
        pub trait Encoder: Codec + Sized + BufWrite {
            type Error: EncodeError;
            type TupleEncoder: CompositeEncoder<Error = Self::Error>;
            type StructEncoder: CompositeEncoder<Error = Self::Error>;
            type SequenceEncoder: CompositeEncoder<Error = Self::Error>;
            fn encode_u8(&mut self, v: &u8) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_i8(&mut self, v: &i8) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_u16(&mut self, v: &u16) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_i16(&mut self, v: &i16) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_u32(&mut self, v: &u32) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_i32(&mut self, v: &i32) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_u64(&mut self, v: &u64) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_i64(&mut self, v: &i64) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_u128(&mut self, v: &u128) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_i128(&mut self, v: &i128) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_usize(&mut self, v: &usize) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_isize(&mut self, v: &isize) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_f32(&mut self, v: &f32) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_f64(&mut self, v: &f64) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_bool(&mut self, v: &bool) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_tuple<'a>(
                &mut self,
            ) -> Result<&mut Self::TupleEncoder, Self::Error>;
            fn encode_struct<'a>(
                &mut self,
            ) -> Result<&mut Self::StructEncoder, Self::Error>;
            fn encode_seq(
                &mut self,
                len: usize,
            ) -> Result<&mut Self::SequenceEncoder, Self::Error>;
            fn encode_enum_identifier<T>(
                &mut self,
                value: &T,
            ) -> Result<EnumVariantIndex, Self::Error>
            where
                [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:,
                for<'a> &'a T: Into<EnumVariantStringId>
                    + Into<EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>>,
                T: EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantStringId>
                    + EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>>;
            fn encode_some(&mut self) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_none(&mut self) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_str(&mut self, v: &str) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_bytes(&mut self, v: &[u8]) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn encode_seq_len(&mut self, v: usize) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
        }
        pub trait Decode: Sized {
            fn decode_in_place<D: Decoder>(
                decoder: &mut D,
                out: &mut MaybeUninit<Self>,
            ) -> Result<(), D::Error>;
            fn decode<D: Decoder>(decoder: &mut D) -> Result<Self, D::Error> {
                let mut place: MaybeUninit<Self> = MaybeUninit::uninit();
                Self::decode_in_place(decoder, &mut place)?;
                Ok(unsafe { place.assume_init() })
            }
            fn populate<D: Decoder>(_decoder: &mut D) -> Result<(), D::Error> {
                Ok(())
            }
        }
        pub trait Decoder: Codec + Sized + BufRead {
            type Error: DecodeError;
            type TupleDecoder: CompositeDecoder<Error = Self::Error>;
            type StructDecoder: CompositeDecoder<Error = Self::Error>;
            type SequenceDecoder: CompositeDecoder<Error = Self::Error>;
            fn decode_u8(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u8>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_i8(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i8>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_u16(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u16>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_i16(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i16>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_u32(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u32>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_i32(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i32>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_u64(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u64>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_i64(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i64>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_u128(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<u128>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_i128(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i128>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_usize(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<usize>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_isize(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<isize>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_f32(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<f32>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_f64(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<f64>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_bool(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<bool>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_str(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<&str>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_bytes<'a>(&mut self) -> Result<&'a [u8], Self::Error>;
            fn decode_var_i32(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<i32>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn decode_tuple(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self::TupleDecoder, Self::Error>;
            fn decode_struct(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self::StructDecoder, Self::Error>;
            fn decode_seq(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self::SequenceDecoder, Self::Error>;
            fn decode_seq_len(&mut self) -> Result<usize, Self::Error>;
            fn decode_enum_identifier<T>(
                &mut self,
                out: &mut MaybeUninit<T>,
            ) -> Result<EnumVariantIndex, Self::Error>
            where
                [(); size_of::<Discriminant<T>>()]:,
                T: EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantStringId>
                    + EnumIdentifierToVariantIndex<EnumVariantDiscriminantId<T>>;
            fn decode_is_some(&mut self) -> Result<bool, Self::Error>;
        }
        pub trait EncodeError {
            fn not_enough_space_in_the_buffer() -> Self;
            fn too_large() -> Self;
            fn custom() -> Self;
        }
        pub trait DecodeError {
            fn not_enough_bytes_in_the_buffer() -> Self;
            fn too_large() -> Self;
            fn invalid_enum_identifier() -> Self;
            fn custom() -> Self;
            fn invalid_utf8() -> Self;
            fn nonmax_but_max() -> Self;
            fn nonzero_but_zero() -> Self;
        }
        pub trait CompositeEncoder: Encoder {
            fn encode_element<E: Encode>(&mut self, v: &E) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn end(&mut self) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
        }
        pub trait CompositeDecoder: Decoder {
            fn decode_element<D: Decode>(
                &mut self,
                place: &mut MaybeUninit<D>,
            ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
            fn end(&mut self) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
        }
    }
    pub use traits::*;
}

            }

            type Item = T;

                self.iter()
            }
            fn as_ptr(&self) -> *const Self::Item {
                unsafe { transmute(self) }
            }

                N
            }

        }
        const _: () = {
            impl<T, const N: usize> FieldOffset for [T; N] {
                type Offset = Const<0>;
            }
            impl<C, T, const N: usize> Edge<C> for [T; N]
            where
                T: Edge<C>,
            {
                type First = End<C, Self>;
                type Second = PhantomEdge<
                    C,
                    Self,
                    (Field<Vectored<[T; N]>>, End<C, Self>),
                >;
            }
            impl<T, const N: usize> Len for [T; N] {
                const SIZE: usize = UNSIZED;
            }
            impl<T, const N: usize> Size for [T; N] {
                const SIZE: usize = size_of::<Self>();
            }
            impl<C, S, T, const N: usize> CompoundWrapper<C, S> for [T; N]
            where
                T: Edge<C>,
            {
                type Compound = <Self as CompoundUnwrapper<C, S>>::Output;
            }
        };

                self.as_bytes().as_ptr()

                self.len()

                let mut s = unsafe { transmute::<_, &mut MaybeUninit<String>>(self) };
                *s = MaybeUninit::new(String::with_capacity(len));
                unsafe { s.assume_init_mut().as_mut_vec().set_len(len) };
            }
        }

        use std::mem::MaybeUninit;

        where
            A: Encode,
            B: Encode,
        {
            fn encode<E: Encoder>(&self, encoder: &mut E) -> Result<(), E::Error> {
                A::encode(&self.0, encoder)?;
                B::encode(&self.1, encoder)?;
                Ok(())
            }

mod endian {
    pub enum Endian {
        Big,
        Little,
    }
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::fmt::Debug for Endian {
        #[inline]
        fn fmt(&self, f: &mut ::core::fmt::Formatter) -> ::core::fmt::Result {
            ::core::fmt::Formatter::write_str(
                f,
                match self {
                    Endian::Big => "Big",
                    Endian::Little => "Little",
                },
            )
        }
    }
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::marker::StructuralPartialEq for Endian {}
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::cmp::PartialEq for Endian {
        #[inline]
        fn eq(&self, other: &Endian) -> bool {
            let __self_discr = ::core::intrinsics::discriminant_value(self);
            let __arg1_discr = ::core::intrinsics::discriminant_value(other);
            __self_discr == __arg1_discr
        }
    }
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::cmp::Eq for Endian {
        #[inline]
        #[doc(hidden)]
        #[coverage(off)]
        fn assert_receiver_is_total_eq(&self) -> () {}
    }
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::cmp::PartialOrd for Endian {
        #[inline]
        fn partial_cmp(
            &self,
            other: &Endian,
        ) -> ::core::option::Option<::core::cmp::Ordering> {
            let __self_discr = ::core::intrinsics::discriminant_value(self);
            let __arg1_discr = ::core::intrinsics::discriminant_value(other);
            ::core::cmp::PartialOrd::partial_cmp(&__self_discr, &__arg1_discr)
        }
    }
    #[automatically_derived]
    impl ::core::cmp::Ord for Endian {
        #[inline]
        fn cmp(&self, other: &Endian) -> ::core::cmp::Ordering {
            let __self_discr = ::core::intrinsics::discriminant_value(self);
            let __arg1_discr = ::core::intrinsics::discriminant_value(other);
            ::core::cmp::Ord::cmp(&__self_discr, &__arg1_discr)
        }
    }
    impl Endian {
        pub const NATIVE: Endian = { if false { Endian::Big } else { Endian::Little } };
    }
}
mod mesh {
    mod edge {
        use std::{marker::PhantomData, ops::Add};
        pub trait Edge<C> {
            /// Indicate leaf
            type First: Edge<C>;
            /// Indicate another edge
            type Second: Edge<C>;
        }
        /// Generic type `S` represents a struct containing a edges.
        pub struct PhantomEdge<C, S, T>(pub(crate) PhantomData<(C, S, T)>);
        impl<C, S, First, Second> Edge<C> for PhantomEdge<C, S, (First, Second)>
        where
            First: Edge<C>,
            Second: Edge<C>,
        {
            type First = First;
            type Second = Second;
        }
        impl<C, S, A, B, Rhs> Add<Rhs> for PhantomEdge<C, S, (A, B)> {
            type Output = PhantomEdge<C, S, (Rhs, PhantomEdge<C, S, (A, B)>)>;
            fn add(self, _rhs: Rhs) -> Self::Output {
                ::core::panicking::panic("internal error: entered unreachable code")
            }
        }
    }
    mod end {
        //! TODO  collect Add<End> for Field,Vectored,Enum... -> and make macro for it

            edge::{Edge, PhantomEdge},
            len::Len,
        };
        ///Token for end
        pub struct End<C, S>(pub(crate) PhantomData<(C, S)>);
        impl<C, S> Edge<C> for End<C, S> {
            type First = End<C, S>;
            type Second = End<C, S>;
        }
        impl<C, S, Rhs> Add<Rhs> for End<C, S> {
            type Output = PhantomEdge<C, S, (Rhs, End<C, S>)>;
            fn add(self, _rhs: Rhs) -> Self::Output {
                ::core::panicking::panic("internal error: entered unreachable code")
            }
        }
        impl<C, S> Len for End<C, S> {
            const SIZE: usize = 0;
        }
    }

            }
        }
        impl<C, S, B, T, V> Add<PhantomEdge<C, S, B>> for Enum<T, V> {
            type Output = PhantomEdge<C, S, (Enum<T, V>, PhantomEdge<C, S, B>)>;
            fn add(self, rhs: PhantomEdge<C, S, B>) -> Self::Output {
                ::core::panicking::panic("internal error: entered unreachable code")
            }
        }
        impl<T, V> FieldOffset for Enum<T, V>
        where
            T: FieldOffset,
        {
            type Offset = T::Offset;
        }
        impl<C, T, V> Edge<C> for Enum<T, V> {
            type First = End<C, Self>;
            type Second = End<C, Self>;
        }
        impl<T, V> Len for Enum<T, V>
        where
            T: Size,
        {
            const SIZE: usize = T::SIZE;
        }
        impl<V, C, S, T> CompoundWrapper<C, S> for Enum<T, V> {
            type Compound = Enum<T, V>;
        }
    }
    mod field {
        use std::{mem::MaybeUninit, ops::Add};
        use super::{
            edge::{Edge, PhantomEdge},
            flatten::{Compound, CompoundWrapper},
            leaf::PhantomLeaf, len::{Len, Size},
            prelude::Vectored, r#enum::Enum,
        };
        pub trait FieldOffset {
            type Offset;
        }
        #[repr(transparent)]
        pub struct Field<T>(MaybeUninit<T>);
        impl<C, T> Edge<C> for Field<T>
        where
            T: Edge<C>,
        {
            type First = T::First;
            type Second = T::Second;
        }
        impl<T> FieldOffset for Field<T>
        where
            T: FieldOffset,
        {
            type Offset = T::Offset;
        }
        impl<C, S, T> CompoundWrapper<C, S> for Field<T>
        where
            T: CompoundWrapper<C, S>,
            Field<<T as CompoundWrapper<C, S>>::Compound>: FieldUnwrapper,
        {
            type Compound = <Field<T::Compound> as FieldUnwrapper>::Output;
        }
        impl<T> Len for Field<T>
        where
            T: Len,
        {
            const SIZE: usize = T::SIZE;
        }
        impl<T> Size for Field<T>
        where
            T: Size,
        {
            const SIZE: usize = T::SIZE;
        }
        impl<C, S, A, B, T> Add<PhantomEdge<C, S, (A, B)>> for Field<T> {
            type Output = PhantomEdge<C, S, (Field<T>, PhantomEdge<C, S, (A, B)>)>;
            fn add(self, _rhs: PhantomEdge<C, S, (A, B)>) -> Self::Output {
                ::core::panicking::panic("internal error: entered unreachable code")
            }
        }
        pub trait FieldUnwrapper {
            type Output;
        }
        impl<T> FieldUnwrapper for Field<PhantomLeaf<T>> {
            type Output = PhantomLeaf<Field<T>>;
        }
        impl<S, T> FieldUnwrapper for Field<Compound<S, T>> {
            type Output = Compound<S, T>;
        }
        impl<T> FieldUnwrapper for Field<Vectored<T>> {
            type Output = Vectored<T>;
        }
        impl<T, V> FieldUnwrapper for Field<Enum<T, V>> {
            type Output = Vectored<T>;
        }
    }
    mod flatten {
        use std::{marker::PhantomData, ops::Add};
        use super::{
            edge::{Edge, PhantomEdge},
            end::End, padding::ConstifyPadding, sort::Sorted,
        };
        /// newtype of `PhantomEdge<S, T>` that represents its the root of a struct
        pub struct Compound<S, T>(PhantomData<(S, T)>);
        pub trait CompoundWrapper<C, S> {
            /// Convert `Edge` to be wrapped as `Compound` or not
            type Compound;
        }
        pub trait Flatten<S> {
            type Output;
        }
        pub trait CompoundUnwrapper<C, S> {
            type Output;
        }
        impl<C, S, T> CompoundUnwrapper<C, S> for T
        where
            T: Edge<C, Second: Sorted<Output: ConstifyPadding>>,
        {
            type Output = Compound<
                S,
                <<<T as Edge<C>>::Second as Sorted>::Output as ConstifyPadding>::Output,
            >;
        }
        impl<C, S, S2, A, B> Flatten<S> for PhantomEdge<C, S2, (A, B)>
        where
            A: CompoundWrapper<C, S, Compound: Add<<B as Flatten<S>>::Output>>,
            B: Flatten<S>,
        {
            type Output = <<A as CompoundWrapper<
                C,
                S,
            >>::Compound as Add<<B as Flatten<S>>::Output>>::Output;
        }
        impl<C, S, S2> Flatten<S> for End<C, S2> {
            type Output = End<C, S>;
        }
        impl<C, S, S2, B> Add<B> for Compound<S, End<C, S2>> {
            type Output = B;
            fn add(self, _rhs: B) -> Self::Output {
                ::core::panicking::panic("internal error: entered unreachable code")
            }
        }
        impl<Codec, S, S2, A, B, C> Add<C>
        for Compound<S, PhantomEdge<Codec, S2, (A, B)>>
        where
            A: CompoundWrapper<Codec, S>,
            Compound<S, B>: Add<C>,
            <A as CompoundWrapper<
                Codec,
                S,
            >>::Compound: Add<<Compound<S, B> as Add<C>>::Output>,
        {
            type Output = <<A as CompoundWrapper<
                Codec,
                S,
            >>::Compound as Add<<Compound<S, B> as Add<C>>::Output>>::Output;
            fn add(self, _rhs: C) -> Self::Output {
                ::core::panicking::panic("internal error: entered unreachable code")
            }
        }
    }
